+    def _validate_terminal_state(
+        self,
+        identifier: str,
+        run_dir: Path,
+        state: dict[str, Any],
+        config: Config,
+    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Revalidate a terminal snapshot before trusting its fast path."""
+        def invalid(reason: str) -> None:
+            self._save(
+                run_dir,
+                state,
+                "blocked",
+                block_reason=reason,
+                error=reason,
+                error_category="blocked",
+            )
+            raise FactoryError(reason, "blocked")
+
+        try:
+            plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
+            replan_reason_ref = state.get("replan_reason_ref")
+            if replan_reason_ref is not None:
+                if not isinstance(replan_reason_ref, str) or not re.fullmatch(
+                    r"replan_reason_[0-9]+\.json", replan_reason_ref
+                ):
+                    invalid("terminal state has an invalid replan reason reference")
+                replan_path = (run_dir / replan_reason_ref).resolve()
+                try:
+                    replan_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
+                except ValueError:
+                    invalid("terminal state replan reason escapes the run")
+                if not replan_path.is_file():
+                    invalid("terminal state replan reason artifact is missing")
+                persisted_reason = validate_replan_reason(read_json(replan_path))
+                evidence_ref = persisted_reason["evidence"].get("artifact_ref")
+                if not isinstance(evidence_ref, str):
+                    invalid("terminal state replan evidence reference is invalid")
+                evidence_path = (run_dir / evidence_ref).resolve()
+                try:
+                    evidence_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
+                except ValueError:
+                    invalid("terminal state replan evidence escapes the run")
+                recorded_hash = persisted_reason["evidence"].get("artifact_sha256")
+                if (
+                    not evidence_path.is_file()
+                    or not isinstance(recorded_hash, str)
+                    or hashlib.sha256(evidence_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != recorded_hash
+                ):
+                    invalid("terminal state replan evidence integrity is stale")
+                if evidence_ref.startswith("sol_review_"):
+                    self._validate_replan_evidence(run_dir, persisted_reason, state)
+            tasks = master_plan_tasks(plan)
+            completed = state.get("completed_commits")
+            if not isinstance(completed, list) or not completed:
+                invalid("terminal state has no completed commit history")
+            task_ids: set[str] = set()
+            plan_ids = {task["id"] for task in tasks}
+            for record in completed:
+                if not isinstance(record, dict):
+                    invalid("terminal state has an invalid completed commit record")
+                task_id = record.get("task_id")
+                commit = record.get("commit")
+                candidate_id = record.get("candidate_id")
+                base_commit = record.get("base_commit")
+                if (
+                    not isinstance(task_id, str)
+                    or task_id in task_ids
+                    or not isinstance(commit, str)
+                    or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", commit)
+                    or not isinstance(candidate_id, str)
+                    or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", candidate_id)
+                    or not isinstance(base_commit, str)
+                    or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", base_commit)
+                ):
+                    invalid("terminal state has an invalid or duplicate completed commit record")
+                if task_id not in plan_ids:
+                    invalid("terminal state references a task absent from the master plan")
+                task_ids.add(task_id)
+                if git(Path(config.repo), "rev-parse", f"{commit}^").stdout.strip() != base_commit:
+                    invalid("terminal state commit parent does not match its recorded base")
+                if git(Path(config.repo), "rev-parse", f"{commit}^{{tree}}").stdout.strip() != candidate_id:
+                    invalid("terminal state commit tree does not match its recorded candidate")
+            if task_ids != plan_ids:
+                invalid("terminal state is missing a committed master-plan task")
+            if state.get("commit") != completed[-1].get("commit"):
+                invalid("terminal state latest commit is not bound to completed history")
+            if state.get("current_task_id") is not None:
+                invalid("terminal state retains a current task")
+            if any(state.get(key) is not None for key in ("candidate_id", "pending_commit", "active_attempt_ref")):
+                invalid("terminal state retains an ambiguous candidate or attempt")
+            gaps = self._remaining_master_plan_gaps(plan, state)
+            if gaps:
+                invalid(f"terminal state has incomplete global criterion evidence: {gaps}")
+            evidence = state.get("criterion_evidence")
+            if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
+                invalid("terminal state criterion evidence is missing")
+            completed_by_commit = {
+                item["commit"]: item
+                for item in completed
+                if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get("commit"), str)
+            }
+            task_by_id = {task["id"]: task for task in tasks}
+            validated_reviews: set[str] = set()
+            for criterion_id, item in evidence.items():
+                if not isinstance(item, dict) or item.get("status") != "PASS":
+                    continue
+                commit_record = completed_by_commit.get(item.get("commit"))
+                if commit_record is None or item.get("candidate_id") != commit_record.get("candidate_id"):
+                    invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has stale commit lineage")
+                task = task_by_id.get(item.get("task_id"))
+                if task is None:
+                    invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has an unknown task")
+                for field in ("review_ref", "verification_ref"):
+                    reference = item.get(field)
+                    if not isinstance(reference, str) or not reference:
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has no {field}")
+                    artifact = (run_dir / reference).resolve()
+                    try:
+                        artifact.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
+                    except ValueError:
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has an escaping {field}")
+                    if not artifact.is_file():
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references missing {field}")
+                    artifact_value = read_json(artifact)
+                    if not isinstance(artifact_value, dict):
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references malformed {field}")
+                    recorded_hash = item.get(
+                        "review_sha256" if field == "review_ref" else "verification_sha256"
+                    )
+                    if (
+                        not isinstance(recorded_hash, str)
+                        or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", recorded_hash)
+                        or hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != recorded_hash
+                    ):
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} {field} integrity is stale")
+                    if field == "review_ref" and (
+                        artifact_value.get("decision") != "APPROVE"
+                    ):
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references a non-approval review")
+                    if field == "review_ref" and (
+                        artifact_value.get("base_commit") != item.get("base_commit")
+                        or artifact_value.get("candidate_id") != item.get("candidate_id")
+                    ):
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} review identity is stale")
+                    if field == "verification_ref" and (
+                        artifact_value.get("passed") is not True
+                        or artifact_value.get("returncode") != 0
+                        or not isinstance(artifact_value.get("command"), str)
+                        or artifact_value.get("task_id") != item.get("task_id")
+                        or artifact_value.get("base_commit") != item.get("base_commit")
+                        or artifact_value.get("candidate_id") != item.get("candidate_id")
+                    ):
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references failed verification")
+                    if field == "verification_ref":
+                        verification_match = re.fullmatch(r"test_([0-9]+)\.json", reference)
+                        if not verification_match or artifact_value.get("artifact_sequence") != int(verification_match.group(1)):
+                            invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} verification sequence is stale")
+                        configured_command = config.test_command
+                        if configured_command is not None and artifact_value.get("command") != normalize_test_command(configured_command):
+                            invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} verification command is stale")
+                        log_value = artifact_value.get("log")
+                        if not isinstance(log_value, str) or not log_value:
+                            invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} verification log is missing")
+                        log_path = Path(log_value).expanduser().resolve()
+                        try:
+                            log_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
+                        except ValueError:
+                            invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} verification log escapes the run")
+                        if not log_path.is_file() or log_path.name != f"test_{verification_match.group(1)}.log":
+                            invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} verification log is stale")
+                        if (
+                            not isinstance(artifact_value.get("log_sha256"), str)
+                            or hashlib.sha256(log_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+                            != artifact_value.get("log_sha256")
+                        ):
+                            invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} verification log integrity is stale")
+                        if artifact_value.get("sequence") != item.get("verification_sequence"):
+                            invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} verification result sequence is stale")
+                review_ref = item.get("review_ref")
+                if isinstance(review_ref, str) and review_ref not in validated_reviews:
+                    review_path = (run_dir / review_ref).resolve()
+                    review_value = read_json(review_path)
+                    commit = commit_record["commit"]
+                    committed_diff = git(
+                        Path(config.repo),
+                        "diff",
+                        f"{item['base_commit']}..{commit}",
+                        "--binary",
+                        "--no-ext-diff",
+                    ).stdout
+                    verification_contract = self._verification_contract(run_dir, item["verification_ref"])
+                    validate_review(
+                        review_value,
+                        hashlib.sha256(committed_diff.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
+                        item["base_commit"],
+                        item["candidate_id"],
+                        item.get("review_criteria_ids")
+                        if isinstance(item.get("review_criteria_ids"), list)
+                        else self._task_review_criteria_ids(self._task_execution_plan(plan, task)),
+                        verification_contract,
+                    )
+                    validated_reviews.add(review_ref)
+            ledger = MemoryLedger(
+                run_dir,
+                identifier,
+                config.repo,
+                state.get("objective_class", "capability_growth"),
+            )
+            if any(item.get("status") in {"open", "regressed"} for item in ledger.issues.values()):
+                invalid("terminal state has unresolved ledger findings")
+        except FactoryError as exc:
+            if state.get("phase") != "blocked":
+                invalid(f"terminal state validation failed: {exc}")
+            raise
+        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeError) as exc:
+            invalid(f"terminal state evidence is invalid: {exc}")
+        return state

+    def test_disposable_candidate_sync_rejects_host_creation_race_for_missing_file(self) -> None:
+        mirror = self.root / "candidate-mirror-new-file-race"
+        AgyBuilder._copy_sandbox_candidate(self.candidate, mirror)
+        before = factory_module._snapshot_probe_tree(mirror)
+        baseline = AgyBuilder._candidate_baseline_identity(self.candidate)
+        (mirror / "new.py").write_text("created = True\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        original_create = factory_module._replace_candidate_file
+        attempted = {"value": False}
+
+        def create_host_first(source, target, **kwargs):
+            attempted["value"] = True
+            target.write_text("host-created-first\n", encoding="utf-8")
+            return original_create(source, target, **kwargs)
+
+        with patch.object(factory_module, "_replace_candidate_file", side_effect=create_host_first):
+            with self.assertRaises(BuilderProcessError) as context:
+                AgyBuilder._sync_sandbox_candidate(
+                    self.candidate,
+                    mirror,
+                    ["new.py"],
+                    before,
+                    baseline,
+                    stdout_path=self.root / "stdout-new-file-race",
+                    stderr_path=self.root / "stderr-new-file-race",
+                )
+        self.assertTrue(attempted["value"])
+        self.assertEqual(context.exception.failure_code, "agy_isolation_failed")
+        self.assertEqual((self.candidate / "new.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8"), "host-created-first\n")

+        codes = {
+            "ASSUMPTION_DISPROVED",
+            "REQUIRED_GAP_UNCOVERED",
+            "ARCHITECTURE_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT",
+            "PLAN_EXHAUSTED_OBJECTIVE_INCOMPLETE",
+            "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE",
+        }
+        for code in codes:
+            with self.subTest(code=code):
+                self.assertEqual(
+                    validate_replan_reason(
+                        {
+                            "code": code,
+                            "evidence": {
+                                "artifact_ref": "verified",
+                                "kind": {
+                                    "ASSUMPTION_DISPROVED": "assumption",
+                                    "REQUIRED_GAP_UNCOVERED": "gap",
+                                    "ARCHITECTURE_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT": "dependency_conflict",
+                                    "PLAN_EXHAUSTED_OBJECTIVE_INCOMPLETE": "completion_gap",
+                                    "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE": "user_change",
+                                }[code],
+                                "verified": True,
+                            },
+                        }
+                    )["code"],
+                    code,
+                )
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "unsupported code"):
+            validate_replan_reason({"code": "plan", "evidence": {"artifact_ref": "unknown", "kind": "gap", "verified": True}})
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "exactly code and evidence"):
+            validate_replan_reason({"code": "ASSUMPTION_DISPROVED", "evidence": {}, "extra": True})

+    def test_explicit_replan_trigger_routes_to_astra_with_durable_reason(self):
+        for code in (
+            "ASSUMPTION_DISPROVED",
+            "REQUIRED_GAP_UNCOVERED",
+            "ARCHITECTURE_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT",

+                config = self.config()
+                config.repo = str(repo)
+                identifier = engine.plan(config)
+                state = engine.execute(identifier)
+                self.assertEqual(state["phase"], "complete")
+                self.assertEqual(roles.astra_invocation_count, 2)
+                reason_files = sorted((root / "state" / identifier).glob("replan_reason_*.json"))
+                self.assertEqual(len(reason_files), 1)
+                reason_value = json.loads(reason_files[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+                self.assertEqual(reason_value["code"], code)
+                if code == "ASSUMPTION_DISPROVED":
+                    review_path = root / "state" / identifier / reason_value["evidence"]["artifact_ref"]
+                    review_path.write_text(review_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+                    with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "terminal state"):
+                        engine.status(identifier)

+    def test_fabricated_replan_artifact_cannot_invoke_astra(self):
+        roles, builder = FakeRoles(), FakeBuilder()

+        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())

+            run_dir / "fabricated.json",

+                "producer": "Runner",
+                "assumption_id": "AS1",
+            },
+        )

+            "code": "ASSUMPTION_DISPROVED",
+            "evidence": {"artifact_ref": "fabricated.json", "kind": "assumption", "verified": True},
+        }
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "bounded host artifact"):
+            engine.request_replan(identifier, reason)
+        self.assertEqual(roles.astra_invocation_count, 1)

+    def test_replan_budget_survives_crash_between_reason_persistence_and_astra(self):
+        roles, builder = FakeRoles(), FakeBuilder()
+        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=roles, builder=builder)
+        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
+        original_planning = engine._perform_planning
+        crashed = {"value": False}
+
+        def crash_first_replan(*args, **kwargs):
+            if kwargs.get("replan_reason") and not crashed["value"]:
+                crashed["value"] = True
+                raise KeyboardInterrupt("crash after replan reservation")
+            return original_planning(*args, **kwargs)
+
+        with patch.object(FactoryEngine, "_record_approved_criteria", return_value=None), patch.object(
+            engine, "_perform_planning", side_effect=crash_first_replan
+        ):
+            with self.assertRaises(KeyboardInterrupt):
+                engine.execute(identifier)
+        self.assertEqual(engine.status(identifier)["phase"], "planning")
+        self.assertTrue((self.state_root / identifier / "replan_reason_1.json").is_file())
+        before_resume_astra = roles.astra_invocation_count
+        with patch.object(FactoryEngine, "_record_approved_criteria", return_value=None):
+            with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "cannot produce a ready task"):
+                engine.execute(identifier)
+        state = engine.status(identifier)
+        self.assertEqual(state["phase"], "blocked")
+        self.assertEqual(roles.astra_invocation_count, before_resume_astra + 2)
+        self.assertTrue((self.state_root / identifier / "replan_reason_2.json").is_file())
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "blocked run"):
+            engine.resume(identifier)
+        self.assertEqual(roles.astra_invocation_count, before_resume_astra + 2)
+
+    def test_replan_budget_survives_two_crashes_after_astra_responses(self):
+        roles, builder = FakeRoles(), FakeBuilder()
+        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=roles, builder=builder)
+        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
+        original_planning = engine._perform_planning
+        crashed = {"count": 0}
+
+        def plan_then_crash(*args, **kwargs):
+            result = original_planning(*args, **kwargs)
+            if kwargs.get("replan_reason") and crashed["count"] < 2:
+                crashed["count"] += 1
+                raise KeyboardInterrupt("crash after Astra response")
+            return result
+
+        with patch.object(FactoryEngine, "_record_approved_criteria", return_value=None), patch.object(
+            engine, "_perform_planning", side_effect=plan_then_crash
+        ):
+            with self.assertRaises(KeyboardInterrupt):
+                engine.execute(identifier)
+            self.assertEqual(engine.status(identifier)["phase"], "planned")
+            self.assertTrue((self.state_root / identifier / "replan_dispatch_1.json").is_file())
+            with self.assertRaises(KeyboardInterrupt):
+                engine.execute(identifier)
+        self.assertEqual(crashed["count"], 2)
+        self.assertTrue((self.state_root / identifier / "replan_dispatch_2.json").is_file())
+        before_third_execute = roles.astra_invocation_count
+        with patch.object(FactoryEngine, "_record_approved_criteria", return_value=None):
+            with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "replan budget exhausted"):
+                engine.execute(identifier)
+        self.assertEqual(roles.astra_invocation_count, before_third_execute)
+        self.assertEqual(engine.status(identifier)["phase"], "blocked")
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "blocked run"):
+            engine.execute(identifier)
+        self.assertEqual(roles.astra_invocation_count, before_third_execute)

 6384:             raise FactoryError("verification artifact is malformed", "state")
 6385:         required = ("artifact_sequence", "sequence", "command", "returncode", "passed", "log_sha256", "log")

 6499:     def _durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
 6500:         values: list[int] = []
 6501:         for path in run_dir.glob("replan_reason_*.json"):

 6523:         review_ref: str, review: dict[str, Any], state: dict[str, Any]
 6524:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 6525:         original = validate_replan_reason(review.get("replan_reason"))

 6528:         evidence["artifact_ref"] = review_ref
 6529:         evidence["review_ref"] = review_ref
 6530:         evidence["producer"] = "Sol Reviewer"
 6531:         evidence["run_id"] = state.get("run_id")
 6532:         evidence["master_plan_ref"] = state.get("master_plan_ref")
 6533:         evidence["request_ref"] = state.get("request_ref")
 6534:         evidence["task_id"] = state.get("current_task_id")

 6536:         evidence["candidate_id"] = review.get("candidate_id")
 6537:         evidence["candidate_ref"] = state.get("candidate_ref")
 6538:         evidence["verification_ref"] = state.get("verification_ref")
 6539:         evidence["verification"] = review.get("verification")
 6540:         match = re.fullmatch(r"sol_review_([0-9]+)\.json", review_ref)
 6541:         evidence["sequence"] = int(match.group(1)) if match else None

 6544:     def _validate_replan_evidence(
 6545:         self, run_dir: Path, reason: dict[str, Any], state: dict[str, Any]
 6546:     ) -> str:
 6547:         evidence = reason["evidence"]
 6548:         artifact_ref = evidence.get("artifact_ref")
 6549:         if not isinstance(artifact_ref, str) or not re.fullmatch(

 6553:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence is not a bounded host artifact", "blocked")

 6567:             valid = (
 6568:                 artifact_ref == state.get("request_ref")

 6786:     def _block_replan_exhaustion(
 6787:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], *, attempts: int, reason: dict[str, Any] | None = None
 6788:     ) -> None:
 6789:         block_reason = "exceptional replan budget exhausted; run is blocked"
 6790:         evidence = {
 6791:             "reason": block_reason,
 6792:             "replan_attempts": attempts,
 6793:             "last_reason": reason,
 6794:             "last_reason_ref": state.get("replan_reason_ref"),
 6795:         }
 6796:         atomic_json(run_dir / "replan_blocked.json", evidence)
 6797:         self._save(
 6798:             run_dir,
 6799:             state,
 6800:             "blocked",
 6801:             block_reason=block_reason,
 6802:             error=block_reason,
 6803:             error_category="blocked",
 6804:             replan_block_evidence="replan_blocked.json",
 6805:         )
 6806:         raise FactoryError(block_reason, "blocked")

 6808:     def _persist_replan_reason(
 6809:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], reason: dict[str, Any]
 6810:     ) -> str:
 6811:         reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6812:         artifact_ref = reason["evidence"]["artifact_ref"]
 6813:         artifact_path = (run_dir / artifact_ref).resolve()
 6814:         try:
 6815:             artifact_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 6816:         except ValueError as exc:
 6817:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence reference escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 6818:         if not artifact_path.is_file():
 6819:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6820:         evidence_hash = self._validate_replan_evidence(run_dir, reason, state)
 6821:         reason["evidence"]["artifact_sha256"] = evidence_hash
 6822:         attempt = self._durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir, state) + 1
 6823:         if self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir) >= 2:
 6824:             self._block_replan_exhaustion(
 6825:                 run_dir,
 6826:                 state,
 6827:                 attempts=self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir),
 6828:                 reason=reason,
 6829:             )
 6830:         reason_ref = f"replan_reason_{attempt}.json"
 6831:         reason_path = run_dir / reason_ref
 6832:         if reason_path.exists():
 6833:             raise FactoryError("replan reason history is ambiguous", "blocked")
 6834:         atomic_json(reason_path, reason)
 6835:         self._save(run_dir, state, "planning", replan_reason_ref=reason_ref)
 6836:         return reason_ref
 6837: 
 6838:     def request_replan(
 6839:         self,
 6840:         identifier: str,
 6841:         reason: dict[str, Any],
 6842:         *,
 6843:         objective: str | None = None,
 6844:         requested_files: list[str] | None = None,
 6845:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
---6840-6935---
 6840:         identifier: str,
 6841:         reason: dict[str, Any],
 6842:         *,
 6843:         objective: str | None = None,
 6844:         requested_files: list[str] | None = None,
 6845:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 6846:         """Accept only a verified structured strategic trigger for an Astra replan."""
 6847:         normalized_reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6848:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 6849:         with _run_state_lock(run_dir):
 6850:             run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 6851:             if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 6852:                 return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 6853:             if state.get("candidate_id") is not None or state.get("pending_commit") is not None:
 6854:                 raise FactoryError("cannot replan with an ambiguous candidate or pending commit", "blocked")
 6855:             self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 6856:             if normalized_reason["code"] == "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE":
 6857:                 if not isinstance(objective, str) or not objective.strip() or requested_files is None:
 6858:                     raise FactoryError(
 6859:                         "user objective/scope replan requires replacement objective and authorized files",
 6860:                         "input",
 6861:                     )
 6862:                 replacement_files = normalized_paths(requested_files)
 6863:                 repo = self._repo(config.repo)
 6864:                 ensure_paths_confined(repo, replacement_files)
 6865:                 ensure_head_paths_not_symlinks(repo, replacement_files)

 7137:         def invalid(reason: str) -> None:
 7138:             self._save(

 7151:             if replan_reason_ref is not None:
 7152:                 if not isinstance(replan_reason_ref, str) or not re.fullmatch(
 7153:                     r"replan_reason_[0-9]+\.json", replan_reason_ref
 7154:                 ):

 9120:     def status(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 9121:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 9122:         state = read_run_state(run_dir)
 9123:         if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 9124:             config = load_config_for_execution(state)
 9125:             return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 9126:         return state

 9128:     def resume(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 9129:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 9130:         with _run_state_lock(run_dir):
 9131:             return self._resume_locked(identifier)
 9132: 
 9133:     def _resume_locked(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 9134:         run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 9135:         if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 9136:             return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 9137:         if state["phase"] == "blocked" and state.get("error_category") == "blocked":
 9138:             raise FactoryError(
 9139:                 "blocked run requires a resolved condition or explicit authority before resume",
 9140:                 "blocked",
 9141:             )
 9142:         if state["phase"] == "committing" or state.get("pending_commit") is not None:
 9143:             plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 9144:             try:
 9145:                 self._reconcile_pending_commit(identifier, run_dir, state, config, plan)
 9146:             except FactoryError as exc:
 9147:                 self._save(
 9148:                     run_dir,
 9149:                     state,
 9150:                     "blocked",
 9151:                     block_reason=str(exc),
 9152:                     error=str(exc),
 9153:                     error_category="blocked",
 9154:                 )
 9155:                 raise
 9156:             return self._execute_locked(identifier)
 9157:         dispatch = state.get("builder_dispatch")
 9158:         if isinstance(dispatch, dict) and dispatch.get("state") == "dispatched":
 9159:             raise FactoryError(
 9160:                 "run has an ambiguous Builder dispatch and cannot be resumed safely",

 6838:     def request_replan(
 6839:         self,
 6840:         identifier: str,
 6841:         reason: dict[str, Any],
 6842:         *,
 6843:         objective: str | None = None,
 6844:         requested_files: list[str] | None = None,
 6845:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 6846:         """Accept only a verified structured strategic trigger for an Astra replan."""
 6847:         normalized_reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6848:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 6849:         with _run_state_lock(run_dir):
 6850:             run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 6851:             if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 6852:                 return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 6853:             if state.get("candidate_id") is not None or state.get("pending_commit") is not None:
 6854:                 raise FactoryError("cannot replan with an ambiguous candidate or pending commit", "blocked")
 6855:             self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 6856:             if normalized_reason["code"] == "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE":

 6858:                     raise FactoryError(
 6859:                         "user objective/scope replan requires replacement objective and authorized files",
 6860:                         "input",
 6861:                     )
 6862:                 replacement_files = normalized_paths(requested_files)

 6864:                 ensure_paths_confined(repo, replacement_files)
 6865:                 ensure_head_paths_not_symlinks(repo, replacement_files)
 6866:                 config = replace(config, request=objective.strip(), requested_files=replacement_files)
 6867:                 state["config"] = asdict(config)
 6868:                 change_ref = f"request_change_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.json"
 6869:                 normalized_reason["evidence"] = dict(normalized_reason["evidence"])
 6870:                 normalized_reason["evidence"]["artifact_ref"] = change_ref
 6871:                 atomic_json(

 6873:                     {

 6881:             elif objective is not None or requested_files is not None:

 6884:                     "input",
 6885:                 )

 7348:     def _commit_approved_candidate(
 7349:         self,
 7350:         *,
 7351:         identifier: str,
 7352:         run_dir: Path,
 7353:         state: dict[str, Any],
 7354:         config: Config,
 7355:         plan: dict[str, Any],
 7356:         task_id: str,
 7357:         worktree: Path,
 7358:         diff_text: str,
 7359:         digest: str,

 7365:         ledger: MemoryLedger,
 7366:         review_sequence: int,
 7367:         finalize_run: bool,
 7368:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 7369:         self._assert_source_unchanged(
 7370:             Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, f"commit_gate_{self._artifact_sequence(review_sequence)}"
 7371:         )
 7372:         self._scope_check(
 7373:             worktree,

 7375:             f"commit_gate_{self._artifact_sequence(review_sequence)}",
 7376:             run_dir,
 7377:         )

 7379:         current_hash = hashlib.sha256(current_diff.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

 7387:             or review.get("decision") != "APPROVE"
 7388:             or review["reviewed_diff_sha256"] != current_hash

"""Step 3 characterization tests for resume, budgets, trust, and step targets."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import sys

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "lib"))

from hermes_factory_v2 import Config, FactoryEngine, FactoryError, MemoryLedger, atomic_json, validate_plan
import hermes_factory_v2 as factory_module
from test_factory_v2 import FakeBuilder, FakeRoles, make_repo


class DispatchObservationBuilder:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[str] = []
        self.state_at_dispatch: list[dict] = []

    def preflight(self, config, run_dir):
        atomic_json(run_dir / "preflight_builder.json", {"ok": True})
        return {"ok": True}

    def run(self, *, worktree, instruction, allowed_files, config, run_dir, label):
        initial_count = sum(item == "builder_initial" for item in self.calls)
        self.calls.append(label)
        self.state_at_dispatch.append(json.loads((run_dir / "run_state.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
        marker = "" if not initial_count else f"# task {initial_count + 1}\n"
        (worktree / "calc.py").write_text(
            f"{marker}def value():\n    return 2\n", encoding="utf-8"
        )


class ResumeContractTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.repo = make_repo(self.root)
        self.state_root = self.root / "state"

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def config(self, **updates) -> Config:
        values = {
            "repo": str(self.repo),
            "request": "Make value return two",
            "requested_files": ["calc.py", "test_calc.py"],
            "test_command": "python3 -m unittest -q",
            "max_debug_attempts": 1,
            "max_review_cycles": 1,
            "test_timeout": 30,
        }
        values.update(updates)
        return Config(**values)

    def test_resume_from_ambiguous_builder_dispatch_fails_closed_before_builder(self):
        roles = FakeRoles()
        builder = FakeBuilder()
        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=roles, builder=builder)
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        run_dir = self.state_root / identifier
        state = json.loads((run_dir / "run_state.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        state.update({"phase": "building", "builder_dispatch": {"state": "dispatched", "label": "builder_initial"}})
        atomic_json(run_dir / "run_state.json", state)

        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "cannot be resumed safely") as ctx:
            engine.resume(identifier)

        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.category, "state")
        self.assertEqual(builder.labels, [])
        self.assertFalse((run_dir / "worktree").exists())

    def test_resume_from_reviewing_fails_closed_without_replaying_builder(self):
        roles = FakeRoles()
        builder = FakeBuilder()
        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=roles, builder=builder)
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        run_dir = self.state_root / identifier
        state = json.loads((run_dir / "run_state.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        state.update({"phase": "reviewing", "active_attempt_ref": None})
        atomic_json(run_dir / "run_state.json", state)

        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "resumable phase (has no current task|worktree is missing)"):
            engine.resume(identifier)

        self.assertEqual(builder.labels, [])
        self.assertEqual(engine.status(identifier)["phase"], "blocked")

    def test_debug_budget_zero_stops_before_repair_dispatch_and_preserves_failure(self):
        roles = FakeRoles()
        builder = FakeBuilder(initial_bad=True)
        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=roles, builder=builder)
        cfg = self.config(max_debug_attempts=0)
        identifier = engine.plan(cfg)

        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "tests still fail"):
            engine.execute(identifier)

        state = engine.status(identifier)
        self.assertEqual(state["phase"], "blocked")
        self.assertEqual(builder.labels, ["builder_initial"])
        self.assertFalse(any(label.startswith("builder_debug_") for label in builder.labels))
        self.assertEqual(len(roles.reviewer_prompts), 0)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(state["test_results"]), 1)

    def test_builder_call_budget_reservation_is_observed_before_dispatch(self):
        """Characterize the pre-Step-10 baseline's missing durable reservation."""
        builder = DispatchObservationBuilder()
        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=FakeRoles(), builder=builder)
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        state = engine.execute(identifier)

        self.assertEqual(state["phase"], "complete")
        self.assertEqual(builder.calls, ["builder_initial"] * 3)
        self.assertEqual(len(builder.state_at_dispatch), 3)
        dispatch_state = builder.state_at_dispatch[0]
        self.assertEqual(dispatch_state["phase"], "building")
        self.assertEqual(dispatch_state["current_task_attempts"], 1)
        final_state = json.loads((self.state_root / identifier / "run_state.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(final_state["current_task_attempts"], 1)

    def test_tampered_ledger_snapshot_is_detected_without_replacing_it(self):
        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=FakeRoles(), builder=FakeBuilder())
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        engine.execute(identifier)
        ledger_dir = self.state_root / identifier / "ledger"
        trajectory_path = ledger_dir / "trajectory.json"
        original = trajectory_path.read_bytes()
        trajectory = json.loads(original.decode("utf-8"))
        trajectory["sync_seq"] = trajectory["sync_seq"] + 100
        tampered = json.dumps(trajectory, indent=2).encode("utf-8")
        trajectory_path.write_bytes(tampered)

        consistent, reason = MemoryLedger.check_snapshot_consistency(ledger_dir)
        self.assertFalse(consistent)
        self.assertIn("snapshot sequence mismatch", reason)
        self.assertEqual(trajectory_path.read_bytes(), tampered)
        self.assertNotEqual(trajectory_path.read_bytes(), original)

    def test_tampered_review_artifact_blocks_terminal_fast_path(self):
        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=FakeRoles(), builder=FakeBuilder())
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        completed = engine.execute(identifier)
        review_ref = completed["criterion_evidence"]["G1"]["review_ref"]
        review_path = self.state_root / identifier / review_ref
        original = review_path.read_bytes()
        tampered = json.loads(original.decode("utf-8"))
        tampered["decision"] = "REJECT"
        tampered["required_changes"] = ["tampered review artifact"]
        review_path.write_text(json.dumps(tampered, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

        observed = json.loads(review_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(observed["decision"], "REJECT")
        self.assertEqual(observed["required_changes"], ["tampered review artifact"])
        self.assertNotEqual(review_path.read_bytes(), original)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "terminal state"):
            engine.status(identifier)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "blocked run"):
            engine.resume(identifier)
        self.assertEqual(engine.status(identifier)["phase"], "blocked")
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(review_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["decision"], "REJECT")

    def test_corrupted_complete_phase_is_revalidated_and_blocked(self):
        builder = FakeBuilder()
        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=FakeRoles(), builder=builder)
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        run_dir = self.state_root / identifier
        state = json.loads((run_dir / "run_state.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        state["phase"] = "complete"
        atomic_json(run_dir / "run_state.json", state)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "terminal state"):
            engine.execute(identifier)
        self.assertEqual(engine.status(identifier)["phase"], "blocked")
        self.assertEqual(builder.labels, [])

    def test_tampered_verification_provenance_blocks_terminal_fast_path(self):
        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=FakeRoles(), builder=FakeBuilder())
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        completed = engine.execute(identifier)
        verification_ref = completed["criterion_evidence"]["G1"]["verification_ref"]
        verification_path = self.state_root / identifier / verification_ref
        value = json.loads(verification_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        value["returncode"] = 9
        value["command"] = "not-the-authorized-runner"
        value["passed"] = True
        verification_path.write_text(json.dumps(value), encoding="utf-8")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "terminal state"):
            engine.status(identifier)
        self.assertEqual(engine.status(identifier)["phase"], "blocked")

    def test_tampered_review_criterion_blocks_terminal_fast_path(self):
        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.state_root, role_client=FakeRoles(), builder=FakeBuilder())
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        completed = engine.execute(identifier)
        review_ref = completed["criterion_evidence"]["G1"]["review_ref"]
        review_path = self.state_root / identifier / review_ref
        review = json.loads(review_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        review["criteria"][0]["passed"] = False
        review_path.write_text(json.dumps(review), encoding="utf-8")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "terminal state"):
            engine.status(identifier)
        self.assertEqual(engine.status(identifier)["phase"], "blocked")

    def test_resume_reconciles_an_interrupted_exact_pending_commit(self):
        roles = FakeRoles()
        engine = FactoryEngine(
            state_root=self.state_root,
            role_client=roles,
            builder=FakeBuilder(),
        )
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        original_command = factory_module.command
        crashed = False

        def crash_before_first_commit(argv, *args, **kwargs):
            nonlocal crashed
            if not crashed and isinstance(argv, list) and argv[:2] == ["git", "commit"]:
                crashed = True
                raise KeyboardInterrupt("injected commit interruption")
            return original_command(argv, *args, **kwargs)

        with patch.object(factory_module, "command", side_effect=crash_before_first_commit):
            with self.assertRaises(KeyboardInterrupt):
                engine.execute(identifier)

        interrupted = engine.status(identifier)
        self.assertEqual(interrupted["phase"], "committing")
        self.assertEqual(interrupted["pending_commit"]["candidate_id"], interrupted["candidate_id"])

        resumed = engine.resume(identifier)
        self.assertEqual(resumed["phase"], "complete")
        self.assertEqual(len(resumed["completed_commits"]), 3)

    def test_resume_rejects_post_commit_tracked_untracked_and_ignored_mutations(self):
        (self.repo / ".gitignore").write_text("ignored-after-commit.txt\n", encoding="utf-8")
        subprocess.run(["git", "add", ".gitignore"], cwd=self.repo, check=True)
        subprocess.run(["git", "commit", "-qm", "add ignored fixture"], cwd=self.repo, check=True)
        engine = FactoryEngine(
            state_root=self.state_root,
            role_client=FakeRoles(),
            builder=FakeBuilder(),
        )
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        original_command = factory_module.command
        crashed = False

        def commit_then_mutate(argv, *args, **kwargs):
            nonlocal crashed
            if not crashed and isinstance(argv, list) and argv[:2] == ["git", "commit"]:
                crashed = True
                result = original_command(argv, *args, **kwargs)
                worktree = Path(kwargs["cwd"])
                (worktree / "calc.py").write_text("post-approval tracked mutation\n", encoding="utf-8")
                (worktree / "untracked-after-commit.txt").write_text("untracked\n", encoding="utf-8")
                (worktree / "ignored-after-commit.txt").write_text("ignored\n", encoding="utf-8")
                raise KeyboardInterrupt("injected after commit before state finalization")
            return original_command(argv, *args, **kwargs)

        with patch.object(factory_module, "command", side_effect=commit_then_mutate):
            with self.assertRaises(KeyboardInterrupt):
                engine.execute(identifier)

        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "post-approval|post-commit"):
            engine.resume(identifier)
        state = engine.status(identifier)
        self.assertEqual(state["phase"], "blocked")
        self.assertNotEqual(state.get("phase"), "complete")

    def test_structured_steps_are_rejected_before_editable_file_sets_can_run(self):
        """Characterize the pre-Step-8 baseline limitation explicitly."""
        plan = {
            "objective": "characterize slices",
            "allowed_files": ["calc.py", "test_calc.py"],
            "implementation_steps": [
                {"step": "change implementation", "editable_files": ["calc.py"]},
                {"step": "change test", "editable_files": ["test_calc.py"]},
            ],
            "acceptance_criteria": ["both files are valid"],
            "recommended_tests": ["python3 -m unittest -q"],
            "risks": [],
        }
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "implementation_steps must contain only non-empty strings") as ctx:
            validate_plan(plan, ["calc.py", "test_calc.py"])
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.category, "contract")

    def test_structured_step_editable_files_outside_plan_are_rejected(self):
        plan = {
            "objective": "characterize scope",
            "allowed_files": ["calc.py"],
            "implementation_steps": [{"step": "escape", "editable_files": ["outside.py"]}],
            "acceptance_criteria": ["never escapes"],
            "recommended_tests": ["python3 -m unittest -q"],
            "risks": [],
        }
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "implementation_steps must contain only non-empty strings") as ctx:
            validate_plan(plan, ["calc.py"])
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.category, "contract")

    def test_failed_run_keeps_ledger_and_last_failure_event_for_resume_diagnostics(self):
        engine = FactoryEngine(
            state_root=self.state_root,
            role_client=FakeRoles(),
            builder=FakeBuilder(violate_scope=True),
        )
        identifier = engine.plan(self.config())
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "scope violation"):
            engine.execute(identifier)

        ledger_dir = self.state_root / identifier / "ledger"
        trajectory = json.loads((ledger_dir / "trajectory.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(trajectory["events"][-1]["phase"], "failed")
        self.assertEqual(trajectory["events"][-1]["result"], "scope")
        self.assertTrue((self.state_root / identifier / "run_state.json").is_file())

 7976:     def _execute_one_task(
 7977:         self,
 7978:         identifier: str,
 7979:         plan: dict[str, Any],
 7980:         task_id: str | None,
 7981:         *,
 7982:         perform_preflight: bool,
 7983:         finalize_run: bool,
 7984:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 7985:         run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 7986:         if finalize_run and state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 7987:             return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 7988:         state.setdefault("luna_invocation_count", 0)
 7989:         completed_for_offset = state.get("completed_commits")
 7990:         completed_offset = len(completed_for_offset) * 1000 if isinstance(completed_for_offset, list) else 0
 7991:         existing_artifact_numbers = [
 7992:             int(match.group(1))
 7993:             for path in run_dir.iterdir()
 7994:             if (match := re.search(r"_(\d+)(?:\.[^.]+)$", path.name))
 7995:         ]
 7996:         maximum_existing = max(existing_artifact_numbers, default=-1)
 7997:         self._artifact_offset = max(completed_offset, maximum_existing + 1)
 7998:         allowed = plan["allowed_files"]
 7999:         ensure_paths_confined(Path(config.repo), allowed)
 8000:         previous_tests = state.get("test_results")
 8001:         tests: list[dict[str, Any]] = list(previous_tests) if isinstance(previous_tests, list) else []
 8002:         previous_checkpoints = state.get("checkpoints")
 8003:         checkpoints: list[str] = (
 8004:             list(previous_checkpoints) if isinstance(previous_checkpoints, list) else []
 8005:         )
 8006:         previous_review_cycles = state.get("review_cycles", 0)
 8007:         if isinstance(previous_review_cycles, bool) or not isinstance(previous_review_cycles, int) or previous_review_cycles < 0:
 8008:             raise FactoryError("run has invalid review cycle count", "state")
 8009: 
 8010:         root_obj_class = state.get("objective_class") or classify_objective(config.request)["classification"]
 8011:         ledger = MemoryLedger(run_dir, identifier, config.repo, root_obj_class)
 8012: 
 8013:         try:
 8014:             self._save(run_dir, state, "preflight_execution", error=None, error_category=None)
 8015:             # All three capabilities are proven before creating or changing the task worktree.
